<a id="source-project-md-l3097"></a>
### 6.5 Discrepancies and comparison discipline

The reference receipts preserve rather than conceal important discrepancies:

- CS-BuFLO prose says to round the median interval up to a power of two, while
  Algorithm 2's formula floors it. The independent oracle follows the formula.
- With an empty estimator window, the paper retains the current rate while the
  pinned author artefact selects the upper rate. Both behaviours have golden
  vectors; the live algorithm follows the paper contract.
- The pinned runtime actively implements CPSP at both endpoints. CTSP survives
  as an inactive/commented source expression and is executed only through the
  isolated source-slice harness.
- The pinned padding-done flag has setters/resetters but no active consumer in
  the inspected client/server loop. Its early-termination state machine and
  exact quiet-boundary comparisons also differ from the paper prose.
- QCSD adapts on fresh application bytes and performs local early termination;
  the author prototype counts transmitted real-plus-junk bytes and assumes
  cooperating endpoints.

Every comparison row must state transport, endpoint cooperation, site/class
count, visits, observation layer, header accounting, padding variant,
early-termination semantics, overhead formula, latency definition, and
classifier protocol. Numeric proximity to a historical table is contextual,
not an acceptance range. Strict acceptance comes from oracle/artefact
conformance, live correctness, and the study's hard gates. An unexplained
difference blocks promotion.

<a id="source-project-md-l3124"></a>
## 7. Campaigns, evaluation, and evidence products

<a id="source-project-md-l3126"></a>
### 7.1 Prospective 100-class cohort

The formerly planned five-workload candidate formal stage has been superseded by the
prospective `classifier-multiorigin100-v1` protocol. The final target is 100
prepared public-page classes, with exactly 20 classes from each of five fixed
Tranco rank strata and 20 additional ordered reserves. It has its own fitting
bundles, qualification sets, campaigns, handoff, evaluator, and evidence
identity.

The pinned Tranco `W36Q9` receipt and deterministic 600-domain catalogue are
present under
[`config/class-study/v1/`](../../config/class-study/v1). Every
catalogue `eligible` value remains false/unassessed: no page has yet passed the
30-second, 24-hour, and 72-hour stability gate, and neither the 120-class pilot
nor the 100+20 final/reserve selection and assembly receipts exist. The
authoritative prospective contract is
[`study.json`](../../config/class-study/v1/study.json), interpreted by
the checked-in coordinator and validators.

The stability stage requires much more than a nominal 72-hour observation
period. Navigation is completed before a baseline is armed. One bounded action
may advance a deterministic compatible pair, but never more than two
candidates or five live page workers. Starting from the catalogue-order anchor,
selection scans later candidates in immutable catalogue order and pairs the
first candidate with the same priority and stage; probe pairs must also share a
probe identifier, and the combined retained page count must fit the five-page
cap. Incompatible intervening candidates are skipped. The anchor runs alone
only when no later candidate satisfies every compatibility condition.
The coordinator publishes its active batch and pending attempts before
parallel work and merges outcomes in immutable catalogue order. A paired
baseline is atomically recorded with one timestamp, and that action waits
locally and interruptibly for its `t+30s` probe, avoiding a second
Docker/status launch inside the ten-second window. A 40-minute reservation is
then enforced between baseline batches and their `t+24h` and `t+72h` action
starts, including cross-offset collisions. Under zero work and the assumption
that all candidates form compatible pairs, 600 candidates form 300 batches:
the last baseline is at 32 d 5 h 20 min and its earliest `t+72h` probe is at
35 d 5 h 5 min after the first baseline. This best-compatible projection is
not a guaranteed duration. When no later
compatible partner exists for an anchor, singleton fallback can raise the count
towards 600. The all-singleton projection places the last baseline at 65 d 11 h
5 min and its earliest `t+72h` probe at 68 d 10 h 50 min (approximately 68
days). Both are deterministic earliest-next greedy
projections, not globally optimal or universal lower bounds; deliberately
delaying an earlier baseline can change the endpoint. Navigation, preparation,
Docker, network, retries, and interruptions add wall time. The reservation
derives from the configured 2,040-second outer cut-off, a 310-second status
envelope, and a 50-second margin; it is a scheduling policy, not a
call-tree-derived guarantee that an acquisition action succeeds. The launcher
rejects a completed navigation or probe ledger attempt beyond the 1,800-second
soft limit, while the whole-process cut-off is externally enforced with
process-status evidence rather than a separate per-action duration receipt.
Direct `acquisition-run` and `acquisition-status` launcher calls lack the
watcher's create-only live-scope authority and fail before Docker access.

The corresponding evidence boundary is acquisition provenance schema 5,
checkpoint schema 2, active-batch schema 1, terminal schema 3, and completion
schema 2. Provenance embeds both the action-timing and baseline-scheduling
contracts at nested schema 2; checkpoints retain an append-only
`baseline_batches` ledger and nullable transactional `active_batch`. Genuine
historical acquisition schemas 1–4 remain readable only for verification and
cannot mutate or publish new evidence. Schema 2 is retained against its
declared intermediate verifier contract but has no committed producer or
artefact; schema 4 introduced bounded two-candidate batching but was superseded
before live execution. Current mutations and publications require schema 5,
which additionally binds the extended foundation and browser-egress boundary.
The immutable public limits are two
candidates per action and five simultaneous live pages. The production watcher
always invokes the coordinator with a maximum of two; one is supported only for
internal and deterministic test use, not as a production tuning control.

Acquisition status distinguishes live probes from deterministic recovery work.
It reports `acquisition_schema_version=5`, `checkpoint_schema_version=2`,
`maximum_candidates_per_action=2`, `global_live_page_cap=5`, and either a null
`active_batch` or a summary with the exact `batch_id`, `stage`, `published_at`,
`candidate_ids`, `live_page_count`, and `attempt_count` fields.
`due_now_count`, `finalisable_count`, and `missed_window_count` are disjoint
subsets of `probing_count`, and their sum cannot exceed `probing_count`; a
finalisable candidate needs only deterministic terminal/publication recovery
and is not a live due probe. `work_due_now` is true exactly when
`recovery_required_count` is positive, any of those three counts is positive,
or `pending_count` is positive while `pending_start_blocked` is false.
Completion requires all 600 candidates terminal,
`recovery_required_count=0`, `finalisable_count=0`, no active batch, zero
pending or probing candidates, and no `next_due` value.

The prospective pool contains 120 domains per rank stratum. That reduces the
minimum technical-eligibility yield needed for each 24-class pilot stratum
from 40% in the superseded 300-domain draft to 20%, without introducing
post-hoc replacement or outcome-based selection.

The class design follows the QCSD paper's 100-monitored-page scale while
improving population coverage through fixed Tranco rank strata. It also adopts
the distinction between a webpage and an entire website emphasised by
[Panchenko et al.](https://www.ndss-symposium.org/wp-content/uploads/2017/09/website-fingerprinting-internet-scale.pdf)
and the observer-dependent feature caution demonstrated by
[Yan and Kaur](https://petsymposium.org/popets/2018/popets-2018-0039.pdf).
Accordingly, the experiment makes claims about exact prepared page classes,
freezes the observer feature boundary, and prohibits separability-driven class
selection.

The class identity boundary is the exact frozen Tranco candidate domain, not a
boundary calculated from a Public Suffix List: only that host and its
subdomains are admissible for primary navigation and redirects. Cross-origin
subresources are deliberately permitted: discovery iteratively expands a
frozen public-HTTPS GET allow-list and preparation requires rendered coverage
from every approved origin. Navigation-origin seeds are isolated per candidate
page rather than unioned across the homepage and optional pages. The limits of
32 approved origins, 512 audited origins, and eight convergence passes are
fail-closed admission bounds; exceeding any limit or failing to converge
rejects the class instead of truncating an accepted workload. Every discovered
**eligible public HTTPS GET** subresource is retained in an accepted,
converged workload. Observed non-GET and non-HTTPS requests are audited
exclusions outside the replay construct; their presence alone neither rejects
the class nor authorises omission of an otherwise eligible resource.
Authentication/account state remains inadmissible. The independent browser
load used by final preparation must reproduce the converged origin set: a
newly observed unapproved eligible HTTPS GET fails the probe and forces
reconvergence on a retry, while the persisted complete-coverage validator also
rejects any `origin not approved` exclusion. Single-origin and naturally
multi-origin pages are both admissible; multi-origin status and origin count
are not eligibility, ranking, quota, or membership inputs, and the realised final-cohort
origin-count distribution must be reported. The formal handoff's verifier-bound
`dataset.json.resource_origin_profile` preserves every class's origin count,
the origin-count histogram, and the single-/multi-origin totals. Selection may
therefore naturally produce any realised origin mix, including
zero multi-origin classes, but it may never exclude a candidate because it is
multi-origin. Every accepted class retains its complete converged approved
origin/resource graph rather than a single-origin projection. That graph is
invariant under successor selection: an activated reserve retains the exact
admitted prepared workload and complete approved-origin and resource graph
from the original acquisition-assembly lineage; origin count remains neither
a replacement input nor a quota. Longitudinal stability compares final
response identity and a semantic prepared-replay hash
over source/final URL, approved origins, expected responses, and the complete
Neqo runtime manifest. Raw
prepared-manifest hashes may differ because they include per-run provenance;
after all three windows pass, the first `t+30s` raw manifest is the workload
admitted to the immutable cohort assembly.

Current synthetic campaign coverage constructs a two-origin document/script
dependency graph and proves that all nine certification cells bind the same
prepared-manifest SHA-256, resource IDs, and derived runtime manifest. The
eight defended cells additionally bind that prepared manifest as
`--application-workload-source`; undefended uses its derived runtime directly.
Loader-level fitting tests independently carry the same complete two-origin
graph through both the 120-class, two-policy, two-visit pilot plan (480
samples) and the 100-class, ten-visit, two-policy authoritative plan (2,000
samples), including the document-to-script dependency and `Referer` request
header. This validates the machinery only; no real class has yet been
acquired, so it does not establish the eventual cohort's realised origin
distribution.

The executed-evidence boundary now independently resolves every accepted
sample from its sealed campaign configuration. It hashes the campaign and
admitted prepared application, rederives the canonical runtime manifest,
checks qualified chaff and mode-appropriate parameter/provenance inputs, then
requires `run.json` to match the exact response status/length/body-digest set
and canonical endpoint-origin set. It also binds seed, request policy, runtime
kind, response ceiling, and the 1,200-byte UDP ceiling. This common path is
used by fitting, pilot compatibility, certification, canary, formal promotion,
incomplete successor certification, export, and deep handoff verification.
Consequently, no defence-specific application-resource filter or one-origin
projection can pass by coherently rewriting only result labels and checksums.

<a id="source-project-md-l3291"></a>
#### Defence-failure repair contract

A defence or QCSD fidelity failure encountered during regression, controlled
qualification, fitting, qualification, compatibility screening, final
certification, a pre-block canary, or formal capture is a release-blocking
implementation defect to preserve, diagnose, and repair at the client. It is
not authority to drop or relabel the class, activate a reserve,
project away an origin or resource, weaken a timing/fidelity/acceptance
threshold, or fit around the failed observation. The failed attempt remains
immutable evidence and the dependent stage stops immediately while the defect
is investigated. Lab records an evidence-level violation as
`StrictDefenseFidelityFailure` and a typed client runner/QCSD execution error
as `StrictClientDefenseExecutionFailure`. Both are terminal: automatic retry
is forbidden, resume preserves the sealed incomplete result, and durable
verification rejects any later attempt after either type.

Permitted repairs remain inside the client-only QCSD boundary: the Rust
defence/controller/transport integration, client runner, and Lab-side
validation or capture integration may be corrected, but ordinary HTTP/3
servers must remain unmodified and no bilateral or symmetric defence protocol
may be introduced. If the unchanged defence contract cannot be met by a
client-side correction, the candidate or campaign stays blocked and the
limitation is reported.

Every such code correction receives a new create-only immutable cohort and
source/image identity. It repeats the build, pinned-CDP, browser-egress,
isolated-reference, timing-stress, regression, code, and controlled foundation
gates, followed by every fitting, qualification,
compatibility, certification, canary, or formal pairing invalidated by the old
source binding; evidence from different source/image lineages is never spliced
into one paired cohort. Under the generational successor rules below, only a
producer-recomputed, same-class `undefended`
`StrictPreparedResponseIdentityFailure` from the sealed incomplete
first-launch certification may authorise class replacement. A
`StrictDefenseFidelityFailure` or `StrictClientDefenseExecutionFailure` never
can.

This release-blocking path is distinct from bounded retries for operational
capture, transient public-network, or other explicitly retryable failures.
Operational retries cannot reclassify a defence/QCSD defect, and retry budget
exhaustion still blocks the campaign. All prerequisite-ordered class-study
capture and resume roles are coordinator-only: a process-local capability
binds the exact campaign/cohort/assembly/successor identity to the coordinator-
verified prerequisite ledger, so generic `run` and `resume` cannot skip gates.

Each stability retry receives a unique, durably checkpointed attempt identity
and create-only preparation output. A manifest orphaned by interruption is
retained for audit but cannot be reused under a later retry timestamp.

The optional Walkie-Talkie feasibility graph does not extrapolate from a class
passing under some unrelated partner. V1 admits only the 60 selected WT6
profiles frozen in the finalised pilot bundle, each with sufficient prefix and
sidecar capacity at both endpoints; all 7,080 untested alternative pairs are
excluded. The final selector must find 50 qualified edges with exactly 20
classes per Tranco stratum or stop before freezing the final cohort.

The old 2,500-sample corpus remains outside this study. Its 12,503-entry
checksum inventory must be deeply verified before and after formal collection.
No class-study operation may rewrite the historical campaigns, results,
handoff, or checksum inventory.

Fresh class-study publication now has one canonical workspace graph. Generated
campaigns live in
`neqo-qcsd-lab/config/classifier-multiorigin100-v1-campaigns/`; workloads stay
in `config/workloads/`; cohort and assembly receipts stay in
`config/class-study/v1/`; numeric, final, and prefix bundles are exact direct
children of `artifacts/`; and the two final named qualification sets live
under `config/chaff-qualification-store/sets/`. The host launcher derives this
graph from `qcsd_lab.class_layout` and rejects alternate or symlinked fresh
roots before Docker launch. Frozen result-local `inputs/` remain a separate,
hash-bound resume and verification graph. The checked-in layout module and its
tests are authoritative for the exact path graph; local prose and example
commands cannot override them.

Every container-launching `class-study` coordinator action first runs the exact
checkout's `qcsd_lab.class_build_admission` module under `/usr/bin/python3 -I`,
before `require_docker` or stale Docker-state recovery. It follows every direct
and transitive current-build authority consumed by that action and requires all
of them to resolve to one canonical build-execution schema-5/build-completion
schema-1 pair, one clean source identity, and the same collection, preparation,
and reference image IDs. Conflicting, rebound, historical, cross-build, or
malformed authority carriers fail before Docker access. Frozen resume authority
is reconstructed from the role-specific result inputs; successor actions are
restricted to their immutable restart plan. This host gate closes build and
image selection only: the in-image coordinator still performs the complete
scientific validation.

Current publication is source-bound rather than path-trusting. Numeric fitting,
prefix derivation, final fitting, named qualification, and final selection all
re-open and verify the exact fitting result root that produced their inputs.
The qualification authority is reconstructed from the deeply validated
foundation and its no-cache build/source identity, then carried with its
canonical digest through qualification sidecars, manifests, parameter loading,
campaign execution, and attestation. Final fitting provenance and final
selection are both schema 2; their schema-1 shapes were never published and are
explicitly pre-publication and non-evidentiary. This prevents a byte-valid
bundle from being rebound to another capture or foundation after publication.

Frozen result trees are relocatable only within that authority contract. A
result-local copy may replace the recorded foundation path, but every other
foundation hash, payload hash, build identity, source field, and transitive
evidence binding must reconstruct exactly. Moving a result tree while its
external build/gate evidence is unavailable therefore fails deep verification
by design; the copied result tree is not a self-contained archive. The
checksum-closed handoff is the separately verified portable research product.
This boundary is covered by a real deep-relocation integration test without
mocks.

<a id="source-project-md-l3399"></a>
### 7.2 Staged matrices

The implementation separates three pre-formal authorities:

- The create-only **foundation attestation** reconstructs the clean no-cache
  build, reference gate, 110/110 browser-egress qualification, pinned-CDP live
  topology probe, mandatory 12/12 one-launch BuFLO timing-stress receipt, 18/18
  regression, regression-bound code gate, and 160/160 controlled qualification.
  It must verify before public-page acquisition or pilot fitting and has no
  promotion authority.
- The create-only **class-readiness attestation** re-verifies that foundation
  and the common source/build lineage, completed acquisition and admission,
  pilot and final fitting/qualification, and 900/900 one-launch
  certification. It is required before canary/formal capture and has no
  promotion authority.
- The create-only **historical pre-snapshot** deeply verifies the protected
  2,500-sample corpus, binds readiness, and must be created after readiness but
  before every canary or formal launch.

Only the later validation attestation can promote the two candidates.

Only Traffic Morphing, WTF-PAD, and Walkie-Talkie consume the separate natural-
traffic fitting visits. FRONT, Tamaraw, BuFLO, and CS-BuFLO retain fixed
algorithm parameters but still pass their applicable runtime and chaff-capacity
qualification gates.

| Stage | Exact matrix | Samples | Formal use |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| Browser-egress qualification | 110 fresh-browser negative and paired positive-control vectors on the fixed internal packet-observed topology | 110 vectors | Excluded foundation prerequisite |
| Pinned-CDP topology probe | Real pinned Playwright/Chromium recursive page, iframe, dedicated-worker and shared-worker topology | 1 live probe | Excluded foundation prerequisite |
| Mandatory BuFLO timing stress | 12 captured two-origin visits × inclusive 100 s minimum plus bounded terminal drain × BuFLO; one launch each | 12 visits / 60,012–72,000 kernel-timed and post-veth-matched outgoing opportunities, including `t=0`; 60,000–71,988 after-`t=0` releases for sensitivity | Excluded prerequisite; not part of the 18-cell regression |
| Nine-mode regression | 2 controlled workloads × 9 modes × 1 visit | 18 | Test-only |
| Controlled qualification | 2 workloads × 4 treatments × 4 bidirectionally conditioned network profiles × 5 visits | 160 | Excluded |
| Pilot fitting | 120 classes × 2 visits × 2 policies | 480 | Fitting only |
| Pilot full qualification | 120 classes × 6 executions | 720 | Qualification only |
| Pilot compatibility | 120 classes × 9 modes × 1 visit | 1,080 | Excluded |
| Authoritative fitting | 100 classes × 10 visits × 2 policies | 2,000 | Fitting only |
| Final full qualification | 100 classes × 6 executions | 600 | Qualification only |
| Final certification | 100 classes × 9 modes × 1 visit | 900 | Excluded |
| Pre-block canaries | 10 blocks × 100 classes × undefended | 1,000 | Excluded |
| Final formal study | 10 blocks × 100 classes × 2 visits × 8 modes | 16,000 | Authoritative |

The timing-stress prelude uses the exact collection image and source, a clean
network, an ordinary HTTP/3 server, and `experiment.json` as its authoritative
checkpoint. Each visit has a fixed 5,001-opportunity prefix per direction and a
bounded 0–999-opportunity terminal-drain suffix. Every outgoing opportunity,
including `t=0`, must carry complete schema-11 kernel-timed sender,
TX-scheduler/TX-software timestamp, qdisc, clock, privilege,
helper-lifecycle, independent router-ingress observation, and item-by-item
reconciliation evidence. The `N−1` after-`t=0` count is retained separately
for sensitivity and historical comparison. Every exact datagram must be
observed inside the unchanged strict adapter-normalised half-open window, with
zero unresolved items, qdisc failures, or capture drops. The logical slot
inventory, not physical terminal-row order, proves outgoing-before-incoming
scheduling. No rejected or incomplete first launch can be resumed or replaced.
The schema-4 checkpoint binds the cohort, source, image, parameters,
network/environment, workload, qualification, chaff, and opportunity contract;
the create-only receipt is an exact sibling of and is bound into current
regression receipt schema 6. Reserved regression schema 5 cannot authorise a
regression.

Controlled modes are undefended, BuFLO, CTSP, and CPSP. The four network
profiles are clean; symmetric 50 ms RTT; symmetric 5 Mbit/s with 25 ms
one-way delay and a 100-packet queue; and symmetric 1% random loss with 25 ms
one-way delay. Both ingress and egress conditioning are applied in isolated
Docker network namespaces.

Compatibility covers all nine selectable modes. `static` is a mechanical
schedule control and is excluded from the formal efficacy corpus. Formal modes
are undefended plus FRONT, Tamaraw, Traffic Morphing, WTF-PAD, Walkie-Talkie,
BuFLO, and canonical CTSP CS-BuFLO. CPSP remains a controlled ablation, not a
tenth mode. Every block has 1,600 samples, and a deterministic cyclic Latin
square counterbalances mode order. Exact endpoint-origin-set overlap is used
only to reorder already-selected workload/visit groups for cooldown separation;
it never changes eligibility, rank, quota, or cohort membership. The resulting
order respects each approved origin's 30-second cooldown.

The client timeout is 120 seconds, capture timeout 180 seconds, response
ceiling 1 MiB, capture ceiling 64 MiB, cooldown 30 seconds, and settle time one
second. Fitting, pilot compatibility, canaries, and formal capture permit up
to three attempts. Final certification permits exactly one started launch.
Before result materialisation, a global create-only class-study launch
claim fixes one result root for the canonical campaign role/block and exact
cohort/assembly. If no `experiment.json` exists, only recognised pre-experiment
frozen inputs at that already claimed root may be recreated after interruption;
the interruption cannot create a second launch namespace. The retained
`first-launch` receipt name describes that unique campaign-result namespace,
not a one-attempt limit for each canary or formal sample.

A physical sample launch starts when the runner marks it `running`, increments
the durable attempt count, and atomically writes `experiment.json` before the
collector is called. A stop after this checkpoint consumes the launch even if
no attempt receipt was later written. `experiment.json` is the sole
sample-state and resume authority, so an existing experiment must be resumed
at its claimed root. Failed attempts are preserved. An accepted sample is
never deleted, substituted, relabelled, or rewritten. Capacity preflight
deep-verifies the exact sealed 900-cell certification before every canary or
formal launch/resume, derives bytes and elapsed seconds per sample from its
closed evidence, projects the remaining 1,000 canary plus 16,000 formal cells,
and requires three times the projected remaining storage. The class study has
no rehearsal stage and does not infer this projection from the older focused-
study rehearsals. That campaign-scale requirement is separate from the 64 GiB
Docker-data backing-volume minimum enforced for each fresh evidentiary build;
satisfying either gate does not satisfy the other.

Any post-certification change to code, parameters, workloads, chaff,
acceptance rules, formulas, attacks, or manifests fails the base v1 lineage
closed. The 20 ordered reserves are frozen attrition/planning evidence, not an
automated replacement queue, and no predecessor receipt is mutated in place.

A create-only successor decision can be reconstructed only from a sealed,
incomplete, exact 900-cell first-launch certification. The sole authorising
failure is an `undefended` `fidelity`/`StrictPreparedResponseIdentityFailure`
whose details name that same cell's `workload_id`. The identical typed,
same-workload failure under a defended mode is admissible only as
non-authorising corroboration for a class already carrying that undefended
proof. It cannot add a replacement class. A `StrictDefenseFidelityFailure`, a
`StrictClientDefenseExecutionFailure`, a defended-only or unrelated prepared-
identity failure, every other correctness failure, and any capture,
interruption, infrastructure, or unclassified failure block the whole
decision.

Successors are generational. Every generation retains the original frozen
120-class pilot and qualified one-to-one 60-edge graph as selection authority;
binds the exact immediate predecessor cohort, assembly, final selection,
foundation, source, build, and restart lineage; permanently excludes the
cumulative union of all proven failed classes; maximises retention from the
immediate predecessor; and receives a distinct hash-derived study and launch
namespace. Each generation must acquire fresh 2,000-sample authoritative
fitting, 600-execution final qualification, and 900-cell first-launch
certification evidence. Predecessor outputs cannot be reused. Quota or reserve
exhaustion fails closed for a new acquisition cohort, and replacement is
forbidden after authorised formal capture starts.

Within a sealed incomplete certification, every accepted cell under all nine
modes must first pass that full executed-run binding. BuFLO and CS-BuFLO then
receive their additional current-schema algorithm/chronology validation.
Prepared-response failures are not trusted as handwritten labels: the
successor validator recomputes each one from the frozen workload and retained
`attempt-001`, and exact-compares the producer output before it can contribute
to the narrowly typed replacement decision.

Every schema-2 campaign binds both `class_study_cohort` and the mandatory
`class_study_cohort_assembly`. The former freezes ordered selection; the latter
binds that selection to the complete admission ledger and exact prepared-
workload SHA-256 for every class. Results freeze them separately as
`inputs/class-study-cohort.json` and
`inputs/class-study-cohort-assembly.json`, and every result freezes its
create-only claim as `inputs/class-study-launch.json`. Every class-study result
also binds the same source/build foundation; canary/formal results additionally
bind readiness and the historical pre-snapshot. A missing receipt, hash
mismatch, prepared workload differing from assembly evidence, or
launch/authority substitution is a hard failure at campaign load, fitting,
result verification, handoff, and evaluation. The formal handoff carries ten
distinct block launch bindings.

<a id="source-project-md-l3555"></a>
### 7.3 Accepted-sample and handoff contracts

An accepted sample contains exactly:

```text
capture.pcapng
neqo/run.json
neqo/packets.csv
neqo/events.csv
neqo/schedule.csv
```

The direct PCAPNG, runner receipt, and three CSVs are checksum-bound by the
result. Each formal block is verified and sealed before the next. The create-
only study handoff contains raw PCAPNG, a classic-PCAP conversion, raw runner
receipt, identifier-free shape PCAP, canonical classifier-facing CSV trace,
and diagnostic runner CSVs. The shape projection retains only relative time,
client-relative direction, and observer-frame length; it is not a valid QUIC
transcript.

The handoff dataset retains the distinct `class_cohort`,
`class_cohort_assembly`, and `class_study_launches` provenance objects. The
assembly binds the copied `inputs/class-study-cohort-assembly.json` and exact
admitted workload hashes; the launch inventory binds one distinct create-only
claim for each of the ten formal blocks. None can be inferred from, or
replaced by, another.

Handoff schema 3 also places a nine-field `input_bindings` object on every
sample row: campaign, prepared application, derived runtime workload,
chaff-qualification, chaff-manifest, defence-parameter, defence-provenance,
response-ceiling, and UDP-ceiling identities. Verification rederives that
object from the still-sealed source result and exact-compares it with both the
row and copied run. This applies to baseline and every defended mode, not only
the two candidates.

The nullable schema-3 `kernel_tx_evidence` binding is populated only for a
schema-11 BuFLO row. It checksum-copies the three raw kernel-TX sidecar files
into a separate `kernel-tx-evidence/block-XX/<sample>/` subtree and binds the
embedded receipt identity plus source and copy paths and digests. Missing,
tampered, substituted, extra, or non-BuFLO sidecars fail verification. This
addition deliberately leaves the accepted source sample's five-file inventory
unchanged.

The retained focused 1,500-sample workflow has a distinct handoff schema 2;
it must not be confused with class-handoff schema 3. Its nullable
`kernel_tx_evidence` row field copies exactly
`router-capture.pcapng`, `router-receipt.json`, and
`kernel-tx-evidence.json` into
`kernel-tx-evidence/<sample>/`. Those files are closed by `SHA256SUMS`, their
source seal and copied hashes are checked, and deep verification replays the
kernel-TX evidence against the copied `run.json`, router receipt, and router
capture. Historical focused schema-1 handoffs remain readable only for their
pinned non-formal role; a new formal focused handoff requires schema 2 and
reopens its original sealed result roots before it can authorise evaluation,
even when optional deep replay is disabled. This implementation has local test
coverage but no current-source handoff has been exported.

For current BuFLO and CS-BuFLO rows, export reopens the copied raw `run.json`,
`schedule.csv`, `events.csv`, and `packets.csv` before the create-only handoff
directory is published. It rederives terminal outcomes, flat counters,
direction and policy identities, packet composition, incoming-credit clocks,
and stop/drain chronology. BuFLO additionally requires a one-to-one exact
outgoing packet for every slot and independently enforces both controller and
packet lateness inside `[release, release + W)`, with the receipted 5 ms aligned
or 4.999 ms fractional adapter window. CS-BuFLO requires one-to-one
full/partial packet evidence; an incoming packet may be observed but may never
claim a locally scheduled slot. A coherently resealed invalid candidate tree
therefore fails while still staged and cannot leave an immutable destination.

<a id="source-project-md-l3624"></a>
### 7.4 Correctness, performance, and attacks

Client correctness is exact: every resource's status, redirect sequence, body
length, and body SHA-256 must equal the undefended prepared reference. STREAM
bytes may not be lost, duplicated, reordered, produced from chaff, or sent
outside the selected policy. Protocol errors, deadlocks, timeout leakage,
unclosed streams, truncation, clock repair, unexplained endpoint traffic, UDP
payloads above 1,200 bytes, or runner/capture timestamp disagreement above 10
ms are failures.

The evaluator reports, by mode, workload, acquisition block, and direction:

- ratio-of-sums wire bandwidth and additional overhead percentage;
- UDP-payload ratio, packet count, goodput, retransmissions, completion-time
  ratio, added seconds, and p50/p90/p95 tails;
- paired per-visit distributions and 95% block/workload bootstrap intervals
  using 10,000 draws;
- client user/system CPU, wall time, maximum RSS, voluntary/involuntary context
  switches, timer wakeups, and retransmission evidence;
- target-size realisation, interval error/jitter, rate transitions, padding
  thresholds, congestion outcomes, and packet composition.

RAPL energy is nullable and must say unavailable when WSL does not expose it.
The evaluator never replaces a paper's aggregate ratio of sums with a mean of
per-trace ratios.

Attack evaluation covers Panchenko, pinned-reference Weka VNG++, and a
separately receipted clean-room DLSVM/OSA implementation. The primary protocol
uses blocks 1–8 for training, block 9 for validation, and block 10 once for
held-out testing, with no tuning on block 10. It reports adaptive attackers
trained and tested separately within each defence and an undefended-trained
transfer view for continuity. A secondary result is explicitly labelled
paper-style stratified ten-fold. Accuracy, balanced accuracy, confusion
matrices, per-class recall, and 95% block/workload bootstrap intervals are
required. The prospective 100-class study has a 1% random-chance baseline;
the completed historical five-class pilot retains its separate 20% baseline.

## 8. Current candidate evidence and remaining gates

### 8.1 Older completed failed checkpoint: cohort v31

V31 binds clean Lab `5decf36b2806849174e77e183ca7138c743b7d9a`, Rust
`e0179ce1bb18110eacc8fa78f48fc0b6a78dc12d`, and collection image
`sha256:7c9339f11385b2d9dc8dac771e668b6ab8cdc243d76ddb3404d904c364f3bba9`.
It failed its first controlled profile and cannot authorise the post-v31
source. V32 is the newer preserved failed lineage described in section 8.4.

| Evidence | Executed result | SHA-256 |
|---|---|---|
| `build-execution-v31.json` (`artifacts/buflo-study/build-execution-v31.json`) | Fresh pull/no-cache collection, prepare, and isolated reference images passed. | `e7b5027c4d26492d22222e60b8a6dd70774dd0104425ee1a5011fa2d6ce91692` |

| `controlled-results.json` (`results/buflo-study-controlled-v31/controlled-results.json`) | The clean profile ended `incomplete` at 39/40; later profiles were not launched. | `450d47094020c8374235953e1263334c8f16c16ae0b864c7f609783e2eb6af7b` |

The failed clean cell was BuFLO on `local-large`, visit 4. Its preserved launch
history culminated in a strict incoming-credit fidelity failure; the forensic
checkpoint reports a 9,150-microsecond continuation delay. The sample exhausted
its v31 attempt budget. No controlled-qualification or class-foundation receipt
exists. The unchanged evidence is correctly rejected rather than normalised.

<a id="source-project-md-l3686"></a>
### 8.2 V31 diagnosis and subsequent scheduler generations

The v31 miss showed that an accepted scheduled incoming-credit identity could
remain unrealised after the initial outgoing release drive. Post-v31 Rust now
captures every unrealised slot-owned adapter identity, refreshes legitimate
same-tick stream/endpoint movement, and drives only the owning endpoint. A
transport callback may wake the continuation early; otherwise strict quarter-,
half-, three-quarter-, and terminal-deadline wakes cover the unchanged
adapter-normalised window (5 ms aligned or 4.999 ms fractional). Every owner
drive is handoff-bounded and emits at most one datagram, so
it cannot flush unrelated output or burst catch-up traffic.

Expiry reconciles the complete refreshed inventory but emits exactly one typed
`DeadlineExpired` outcome for the logical slot. An exact slot must stage and
physically advertise its own credit inside its own window; it cannot inherit
unowned parser capacity advertised before the window. Physical socket handoff
timestamps and typed pre-handoff/late-handoff errors keep deadline attribution
fail-closed. Incoming server datagram timing and size remain unavailable and
are not claimed.

Runner-wakeup schema 5 bound those post-v31 continuation semantics. Schemas
6–10 successively changed and strengthened the userspace release path without
changing the release or strict deadline. V36 is the only executed schema-6
lineage, and its retained retry history makes its regression non-authorising.
V39 is the only receipt-bearing schema-7 lineage; v42 is the newest
receipt-bearing schema-8 lineage; v46 is the newest receipt-bearing schema-9
lineage; each stopped within the timing-stress prerequisite. V47 is the newest
receipt-bearing schema-10 lineage. It passed build and reference, then failed
the sole first stress launch when outgoing slot 7060 was dispatched 134,680 ns
after the strict deadline and paired incoming slot 7061 missed.

Current Lab requires runner-wakeup schema 11 for fresh BuFLO candidate
evidence while retaining schemas 1–10 as historical, read-only inputs. Schema
11 replaces the userspace release decision with the kernel-timed sender and
binds it to independent router-ingress observation and item-by-item
reconciliation. Accepted study checkpoints retain separately hash-bound
scheduler-runtime and kernel-transmit evidence receipts.
Lab maps typed client defence/QCSD runner failures to the terminal
`StrictClientDefenseExecutionFailure` class and treats it identically to
`StrictDefenseFidelityFailure` for durable stop/no-retry verification.

The
pre-receipt v37 Docker failure, v38 and v40 formatting
failures, source-bound v39/v41/v42 stress failures, v43 Clippy failure, and v44
pre-receipt code-gate failure, v45–v47 stress failures, and non-evidentiary
v48–v50 build attempts, v51/v52 pre-sample capture failures, v53

pre-receipt pinned-CDP failures advance no
class-study numerator.

The v29 no-cache build reached final workspace Clippy, then failed because the
controller test
`local_et_cancellation_requires_fresh_empty_backlog_before_defense_complete`
measured 101 lines against the configured 100-line function limit.
`build-execution-v29.json` was never emitted. V29 is therefore a preserved
non-evidentiary failed build attempt, not candidate evidence.

After the controller-test split, v30 ran at Lab `9b81447…` / Rust `e0179ce…`.
Its embedded Rust test phases and full workspace Clippy passed, but
Docker/BuildKit then
terminated with `SIGBUS` before the collection image could be exported. At the
failure, host C: had approximately 64 MiB free. Docker's ext4 data VHD reported
I/O errors, aborted its journal, and remounted read-only. No
`build-execution-v30.json` was emitted, so the attempted build contributes no
scientific evidence.

Docker subsequently recovered long enough to produce v31 and v32 evidence,
but failed again during the v33 preparation-image export. At 22:45:55 AEST,
the Docker VM logged write I/O errors on `sde`, an aborted journal, a failed
superblock write, and an ext4 read-only remount. `dockerd` terminated with
`SIGBUS`; the waiting `docker-buildx` client later did the same. No v33
artifact or result file was created. The data VHDX measured 612,543,496,192
bytes, and the Windows C: backing volume was at 100% usage with approximately
1.9 GB free. A controlled Docker Desktop restart stayed in `starting` with
engine `_ping` timeouts and was stopped. The subsequent read-only authoritative
probe found about 2.19 GB available on the same C: volume, below the 64 GiB
evidentiary-build floor.

Following explicit authorisation on 1 September, the damaged Docker content
state was removed rather than repaired. Docker Desktop subsequently
initialised a blank engine, and v34 passed all four backing-volume observations
and the full build/provenance envelope. That successful recovery does not
alter the later terminal regression result.

CPU were valid, and the client recorded zero involuntary context switches.
The terminal attempt is preserved in
`regression-results.json` (`results/buflo-study-regression-v34/regression-results.json`).

qualification did not run. V34 is immutable failed evidence and cannot be
resumed, retried, or used to authorise current source.

The committed post-v34 correction kept ordinary-output admission at two actual
adapter windows before release and moved guard/active-wait entry from one
actual window to two. The configured maxima are the nominal `T−10 ms` fields;
physical boundaries are 10 ms aligned or 9.998 ms fractional, and the deadline
is `release + W`. It neither widens the strict half-open adapter-normalised
realisation window nor permits catch-up.
Runner-wakeup schema 6 bound these semantics for its lineage.
The expected active-wait cost is approximately 50% of one pinned CPU during
canonical BuFLO. V35's no-cache build passed its
backing-storage preflight and the preceding mandatory Rust packages, but the
embedded `neqo-bin` gate stopped at 155/156 because
`run_receipt_keeps_evidence_without_duplicate_workload_fields` still expected

no build receipt or image-IID artefact. Rust `fb699636…` now compares against
`RUNNER_WAKEUP_METRICS_SCHEMA_VERSION`; the exact test passes inside the
pinned-NSS diagnostic image.

V36 subsequently passed its fresh build and isolated reference execution, then
exposed why a retry-capable 18-cell regression was insufficient as a scheduler
gate. Its immutable attempt universe contains two approximately 10 ms capture-
reconciliation rejections and one actual paired outgoing/incoming BuFLO miss
with 1,200 retired incoming bytes and 6.032033 ms guard-exit lateness. Later
retries reached accepted cells, but cannot erase or authorise through those
failures. Runner-wakeup schemas
1–6, including v36's schema 6, are now historical-only.

   attempts, then capture that block's 1,600 formal samples under the same
   bounded-attempt policy; repeat for ten blocks so the same frozen 100 classes
   receive 20 visits under each of eight formal modes and produce
   16,000/16,000 eligible samples;
9. create the historical post-formal snapshot, closed-checksum handoff,
   performance/correctness/attack evaluation, and human comparison review;
10. run fail-closed verification and create `validation-attestation.json` only
    if every gate and evidence hash verifies without waiver.

The promotion gates are reference conformance; code; algorithm/transport;
client correctness; controlled and class-wide qualification; first-launch
certification; complete formal
evidence; performance/security reporting; comparison review; and final
attestation. Any unexplained paper/QCSD difference or any source, parameter,
workload, chaff, acceptance, handoff, or evaluator mismatch blocks promotion.

class-study receipt. V29 failed during the no-cache build before
`build-execution-v29.json` could be emitted, so it contributes no immutable
evidence row. V30 passed embedded Rust tests and workspace Clippy, then failed
with Docker/BuildKit `SIGBUS` before image export; it likewise emitted no
`build-execution-v30.json` and contributes no immutable evidence row.

V33 also emitted no immutable evidence row. At Lab
`2f8e7d3564eaf8bab5e5c9b17c2fb3c1996c6472` and Rust/gitlink
`1c220cdbb49a1837657b6a0c454b8114d3454521`, its clean no-cache build completed
the collection target and reached preparation-image export before Docker's
data filesystem suffered write I/O errors, an aborted journal, a failed
superblock write, and a read-only remount. `dockerd` and `docker-buildx`
terminated with `SIGBUS`; no `build-execution-v33.json` or v33 result file was
created. The partial image state was unauthorised and was removed in the
authorised 1 September Docker reset.

formatting-gate failure: it ran no Rust tests, Clippy, reference, or capture.

passed storage admission but emitted no receipt or image IID and reached no
scientific stage. V41 passed build/reference but stopped terminally on the
first of 12 timing-stress visits; it has no regression or later authority. V42
also passed build/reference and stopped terminally on its sole first stress
launch, leaving every later gate absent. V43 failed before a build receipt at
workspace Clippy and likewise reached no scientific stage. V44 failed before a
build receipt in its independent preparation build and likewise reached no
reference or capture. V45 passed build/reference and then stopped at 0/12 when
its old schema-1 Lab validator rejected a valid terminal-drain suffix in the
sole launched visit; its 18-cell regression never began. V45 is immutable and
cannot be reused. V46 passed build/reference, accepted 2/12 timing-stress
visits, and stopped terminally on the third launch after the schema-9
authoritative/counter divergence; its regression never began and it is also
immutable. Current-source progress remains zero for every capture and

and stopped terminally at 0/12 on the first stress launch; its regression never

<a id="source-project-md-l4897"></a>
### 9.5 Maintenance checklist

When this ledger is updated:

1. recheck both Git heads, worktree cleanliness, and the Lab gitlink;
2. distinguish current source from every older evidence lineage;
3. re-run deep verification of any sealed corpus cited as intact;
4. recompute hashes rather than copying them from prose;
5. update the current-head progress table only from immutable receipts and
   authoritative `experiment.json` checkpoints;
6. preserve mode order, schema versions, metric definitions, and candidate
   language;
7. validate every relative Markdown link;
8. keep frozen local history snapshots unchanged; record future status changes
   only in the current ledger;
9. never edit or regenerate a result, handoff, receipt, workload, parameter,
   reference input, or checksum inventory merely to update documentation.

The current Lab command reference and generic attestation workflow are in
[`neqo-qcsd-lab/README.md`](../../README.md). This ledger controls
status and thesis claims; executed receipts control evidence.
